		icon = coal

		allowed = {

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 1527
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation4
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 3
			owns_state = 1527
			controls_state = 1527
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 1527
			controls_state = 1527
			1527 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_1527_coal_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 41
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 3
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = coal
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			1527 = { set_state_flag = has_1527_templateResource_developed }
			1527 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = coal
					amount = 11
				}
			}
		}
	}

		allowed = {

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 159
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation4
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 3
			owns_state = 159
			controls_state = 159
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 159
			controls_state = 159
			159 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_159_coal_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 54
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 3
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = coal
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			159 = { set_state_flag = has_159_templateResource_developed }
			159 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = coal
					amount = 14
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 595
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation2
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			owns_state = 595
			controls_state = 595
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 595
			controls_state = 595
			595 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_595_tungsten_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 44
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = tungsten
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			595 = { set_state_flag = has_595_templateResource_developed }
			595 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = tungsten
					amount = 11
				}
			}
		}
	}

			2192 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = tungsten
					amount = 7
				}
			}
		}
	}
	

	develop_183_tungsten_deposits = {

		icon = tungsten

		allowed = {

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 183
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation2
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 3
			owns_state = 183
			controls_state = 183
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 183
			controls_state = 183
			183 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_183_tungsten_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 55
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 3
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = tungsten
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			183 = { set_state_flag = has_183_templateResource_developed }
			183 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = tungsten
					amount = 14
				}
			}
		}
	}
